```
## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Encode the deferred-delegation rule durably

## Context

We decided that a delivery-plan node's delegated skill (`planning-and-task-breakdown` for build stories; `product-spike` / `skill-creator` / etc. otherwise) fires **at issue-pickup / build time**, not during the `delivery-shape` run — and that the picking-up agent must be *told* to do it, because skills are instruction sets, not functions that auto-call each other.

Right now that rule lives only in a hand-added "▶ On pickup" line on six issues (ABA-285…290) and a comment on ABA-287. That's the weak form your own `eng-principles-universal` Rule A5 rejects: *a target not continuously verified will drift.* This plan encodes the rule so every future shaped project gets it **by construction** — defense in depth across producer, consumer, and a mechanical gate, with the one tracker-specific piece routed to the adapter repo as a backlog item.

**Confirmed decisions:** adapter rendering → backlog issue in `agent-skills-workflow` (not built here); in-repo edits → one direct prerequisite commit on main.

## The mechanism (three enforced layers + one seam)

| Layer | Where | Guarantee |
|---|---|---|
| **Consumer rule** | `rules/linear-workflow.md` (+ `AGENTS.md` mirror) | Always loaded (global CLAUDE.md import) → every agent picking up any issue, in any repo, is told to honor the delegate. The keystone. |
| **Producer spec** | `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` | Tool-agnostic: defines deferred-delegation semantics + the adapter's obligation to surface `delegates_to` on the emitted issue. The spec D2 is built against. |
| **Mechanical gate** | `bin/walk-delivery-plan` | `delegates_to` becomes a required node key → walk exits 2 if any node lacks a delegate. The existing gate becomes the producer-side fitness function. Also carries the delegate into the manifest. |
| **Seam (deferred)** | `agent-skills-workflow` (other repo) | Renders the manifest's `delegates_to` into the concrete "▶ On pickup" issue line + an adapter check. Backlog issue, not built here. |

Relying on the `delivery-shape` skill prose to "remember" is deliberately **not** a layer — that's the drift this replaces.

## In-repo changes (one prerequisite commit)

### A. `rules/linear-workflow.md` — consumer keystone
In **Issue workflow → On start**, add a bullet after the "Every issue must be either…" bullet:

> - If the issue names a delegate (a `Delegates to` / `▶ On pickup` line), invoke that skill **before writing code** to expand the node into its build tasks — `planning-and-task-breakdown` for build stories, the named skill otherwise. Issues with no delegate, and `ktlo` issues, carry no breakdown step.

### B. `AGENTS.md` — one-line mirror
In **On start of any issue**, add the summary bullet (canonical detail stays in the rule file):

> - If the issue names a delegate (`Delegates to` / `On pickup`), invoke that skill first to break the node into build tasks before coding (ktlo issues carry no breakdown step). See `rules/linear-workflow.md`.

### C. `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` — producer spec (tool-agnostic)
1. Mark the field required: on the schema line (≈L95), change the `delegates_to:` comment to note **required on every node**.
2. Add a new section after *Constraints on the contract* (≈after L220), before *Walkability contract*:

> ## Delegation — timing & surfacing
>
> `delegates_to` names the discipline that owns a node, but it fires **at issue-pickup (build time), not during plan emission.** `delivery-shape` emits the hierarchy and stops; the delegate (e.g. `planning-and-task-breakdown` for a `story`) runs when the issue-class artefact is picked up to be built. Rationale: small batches + certainty-decays-with-horizon — expanding every node's fine-grained tasks at plan time front-loads detail that decays before the node is reached (agentic P8).
>
> There is no programmatic skill-to-skill trigger. "Fires automatically" means the **emitted issue-class artefact carries an explicit on-pickup instruction naming its `delegates_to`**, which the picking-up agent follows (consumer side enforced by `linear-workflow`'s on-start step). The **adapter** binding nodes to a concrete tracker therefore **must surface each node's `delegates_to` on the emitted artefact**; a `ktlo` node surfaces "no breakdown step." `delegates_to` is **required on every node** (the walk-script enforces presence; exit 2 if missing) — this is the one delegation obligation the contract places on the adapter, and it stays tool-agnostic (which skill, not which tracker).

### D. `bin/walk-delivery-plan` — mechanical carry + gate
Three small edits (the front-matter reader already parses `delegates_to` generically):
- **Enforce:** add `"delegates_to"` to `REQUIRED["node"]` (L46). → walk exits 2 if any node omits it.
- **Carry:** add `"delegates_to": nfm["delegates_to"]` to the issue dict in `walk()` (L171–182).
- **Render:** in `render_text()` after the `completion:` line (≈L250), emit `delegates_to: {i['delegates_to']}`. (JSON output carries it automatically via the issue dict.)

## Linear changes

### E. ABA-285 (N04) — tighten acceptance criteria
Add a third "Done when": *the emitted plan sets `delegates_to` on every node (walk-script enforces presence; exit 2 if missing), and the skill's emitted-issue guidance documents deferred, build-time delegation per the contract's "Delegation — timing & surfacing" section.* (Note: enforcement now lives in `walk-delivery-plan`, so this is verifiable immediately — it does not wait on `check-plan-framing`.)

### F. ABA-287 (N06) — repoint the pinned comment
Update the existing decision comment to cite `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` § *Delegation — timing & surfacing* as the durable home, so the rule no longer lives only in a comment.

### G. `agent-skills-workflow` adapter — backlog issue (team ABA, no project, label `repo:agent-skills-workflow`)
Title: *Adapter: surface node `delegates_to` as an on-pickup instruction on the emitted issue.*
Body: render each node's `delegates_to` (carried in the walk-delivery-plan manifest) into the emitted Linear issue as an `▶ On pickup` instruction; add an adapter check that fails if any non-ktlo issue lacks it; ktlo → "no breakdown step." Reference the contract section and the six worked-example issues (ABA-285…290) as the target output.

## Execution order

1. Edit C + D (contract + walk-script), then run the walk-script to confirm the example still passes.
2. Edit A + B (consumer rule + mirror).
3. One commit on main: `feat(delivery-shape): encode deferred build-time delegation (rule + spec + gate)`. Push.
4. Linear: tighten ABA-285 AC (E); update ABA-287 comment (F).
5. Linear: create the adapter backlog issue (G).

## Verification

- `./bin/walk-delivery-plan examples/delivery-plans/top-down-delivery-planning/` → exit 0, `Oracle … 3 / 9 / 19 -> MATCH`, and each ISSUE block now prints a `delegates_to:` line.
- Gate proof (scratch only, do not commit): on a copy, delete a node's `delegates_to` → walk exits 2 with a "missing required front-matter" error. Confirms the fitness function bites.
- `grep` the new bullet in `rules/linear-workflow.md` and `AGENTS.md`.
- `grep` the new section in the contract; confirm it names **no tracker** (says "adapter" / "issue-class", never "Linear") — tool-agnostic rule preserved.
- ABA-285 shows the third Done-when; ABA-287 comment cites the contract section; the agent-skills-workflow backlog issue exists with the repo label.
- The six existing issues (ABA-285…290) already carry the on-pickup line — they stand as the worked example of adapter output; no change.

## Out of scope

- Building the adapter rendering itself (→ item G, other repo).
- `check-plan-framing` (built later in N04) — framing checks stay there; delegate-presence enforcement lives in `walk-delivery-plan`, so it isn't blocked on that script existing.
```

81	layer:        deliverable | node                   # which layer this file is

83	type:         <node-type>                          # NODE FILES ONLY — the polymorphic node discriminator (see vocabulary); `capability` is one of these types

92	  form:       <completion-criterion form>          # the type-appropriate form (see vocabulary)
93	  criterion:  <the observable criterion, by form>

95	delegates_to: <rule or skill that owns this node type's discipline>
96	```

```
224	## Walkability contract (what a deterministic reader may rely on)
225	
226	This is the half of the kill condition the gate issue tests with an actual walk-script. A reader
227	may assume, without parsing prose:
228	
229	1. **Layers are directory depth.** `D*/` = deliverable, `D*/N*.md` = node.
230	2. **Front-matter is the source of structured truth.** Every layer file opens with a `---` YAML
231	   block carrying at least `layer`, `id`, and (for nodes) `type` + `completion.form`.
232	3. **The manifest is countable.** milestones = `D*` directories; issues = `N*.md` files;
233	   sub-issues = `- [ ]`/`- [x]` lines inside node files.
234	4. **The oracle is the README.** The derived manifest is correct iff its counts equal the
235	   hand-count manifest in the root README; a mismatch means drift or a non-deterministic layout.
236	
237	If a reader cannot satisfy 1–4 mechanically, the standalone-markdown premise has failed and the
238	seam must be re-shaped before the skill is built (the initiative kill condition).
```

```json
  "description": "Decompose a committed initiative into deliverables → nodes → tasks, with every story node carrying acceptance criteria **by default** — so users stop getting low-level tasks missing the \"how do I know this is done?\" check.\n\n**Serves:** D2 → KR2 (the bet).\n\n> **▶ On pickup — before writing code:** first invoke `planning-and-task-breakdown` to expand this node into its ordered, walking-skeleton-first, individually-verifiable build tasks. The checklist below is the node-level scope from the delivery plan, **not** the build sequence — PTB produces that. Then build under `source-driven-development` (the `skill:` label).\n\n**Story**\nAs an agent invoking delivery-shape on a committed initiative, I want the skill to decompose goal + KRs into deliverables → nodes → tasks, with every `story` node carrying acceptance criteria emitted by default, so that I stop getting low-level tasks that are missing the \"how do I know this is done?\" check.\n\n**Acceptance criteria** (this issue's Done)\n\n* Done when: given a fed initiative (goal + KRs as text), the skill emits a file-set whose every `story` node carries an Acceptance-criteria block — checked by `bin/check-plan-framing`.\n* Done when: every deliverable carries a `serves_kr` tag and every node carries a `type` tag, so the hierarchy is walkable (N02's script succeeds on the emitted plan).\n* Done when: the emitted plan sets `delegates_to` on **every** node — `bin/walk-delivery-plan` enforces presence (exit 2 if any node omits it) — and the skill's emitted-issue guidance documents deferred, build-time delegation per `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` § *Delegation — timing & surfacing*. (Enforcement lives in the walk-script, so this is verifiable now; it does not wait on `check-plan-framing`.)\n\n**Tasks** (node-level scope — expand via PTB at pickup)\n\n- [ ] `skeleton` — Decompose one fed initiative end-to-end into the three layers on the thinnest path (one deliverable, one story node with AC) so the full pipeline runs before breadth is added.\n- [ ] Emit acceptance criteria by default on every story node; fail loudly if a story has none.\n\n**Blocked by:** ready — N02 (<issue id=\"a0c62c58-38cb-4102-bbe4-f0013234c52e\">ABA-282</issue>, walk-script) is Done.\n**Delegates to:** `planning-and-task-breakdown` (per-node task breakdown).\n**Source:** `examples/delivery-plans/top-down-delivery-planning/D2-delivery-shape-skill-framing-baked-in/N04-decompose-with-acceptance-criteria.md`"
}
```

```
{"comments":[{"id":"330c06ff-1d81-4858-a5fe-a003be083373","body":"**Design decision — deferred, build-time delegation (pinned so it isn't re-litigated mid-build).**\n\nDelegation does **not** fire during the `delivery-shape` run. `delivery-shape` emits the deliverable→node hierarchy to the tracker (milestones + issues) and stops. The delegated skill — `planning-and-task-breakdown` for build stories, `product-spike`/`skill-creator` for the others — fires **at build time, per issue, when the issue is picked up**. Rationale: small batches + certainty-decays-with-horizon (don't expand all nodes' fine tasks up front; most will change), and it matches the `linear-workflow` \"on start of an issue\" step.\n\nThere is no programmatic auto-trigger between skills (skills are instruction sets, not functions). \"Fires automatically\" only means the emitted issue **carries an explicit on-pickup instruction** that the picking-up agent follows.\n\n**Therefore this node (N06) and the emitter (N04) must bake the hand-off into the artefact, not leave it to the agent's memory (agentic P7):**\n- Every emitted issue carries an `▶ On pickup` line naming the skill from its `delegates_to` to invoke *before* building (PTB for stories; the node's named delegate otherwise; none for ktlo).\n- That line is part of `delivery-shape`'s emitted-issue template — not hand-added per issue.\n\nThe six already-created D2/D3 issues (ABA-285…290) have this line added by hand as the worked example of the target output.","createdAt":"2026-05-25T13:08:05.882Z","updatedAt":"2026-05-25T13:08:05.840Z","parentId":null,"resolvedAt":null,"quotedText":null,"author":{"id":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","name":"Anton Babushkin"}}],"hasNextPage":false}
```

```json
{
  "id": "330c06ff-1d81-4858-a5fe-a003be083373",
  "body": "**Design decision — deferred, build-time delegation (pinned so it isn't re-litigated mid-build).**\n\nDelegation does **not** fire during the `delivery-shape` run. `delivery-shape` emits the deliverable→node hierarchy to the tracker (milestones + issues) and stops. The delegated skill — `planning-and-task-breakdown` for build stories, `product-spike`/`skill-creator` for the others — fires **at build time, per issue, when the issue is picked up**. Rationale: small batches + certainty-decays-with-horizon (don't expand all nodes' fine tasks up front; most will change), and it matches the `linear-workflow` \"on start of an issue\" step.\n\nThere is no programmatic auto-trigger between skills (skills are instruction sets, not functions). \"Fires automatically\" only means the emitted issue **carries an explicit on-pickup instruction** that the picking-up agent follows.\n\n**Therefore this node (N06) and the emitter (N04) must bake the hand-off into the artefact, not leave it to the agent's memory (agentic P7):**\n- Every emitted issue carries an `▶ On pickup` line naming the skill from its `delegates_to` to invoke *before* building (PTB for stories; the node's named delegate otherwise; none for ktlo).\n- That line is part of `delivery-shape`'s emitted-issue template — not hand-added per issue.\n\nThe six already-created D2/D3 issues (ABA-285…290) have this line added by hand as the worked example of the target output.\n\n---\n\n**Now encoded durably** (so this rule no longer lives only in this comment) — defense in depth across three enforced layers + one deferred seam:\n- **Producer spec:** `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` § *Delegation — timing & surfacing* is the canonical, tool-agnostic home for the semantics above.\n- **Mechanical gate:** `bin/walk-delivery-plan` now requires `delegates_to` on every node (exit 2 if missing) — the producer-side fitness function (eng-universal Rule A5).\n- **Consumer rule:** `rules/linear-workflow.md` → *On start* tells every picking-up agent to invoke the named delegate before coding (mirrored in `AGENTS.md`). Always loaded via global CLAUDE.md import.\n- **Adapter seam (deferred):** rendering `delegates_to` into the concrete `▶ On pickup` issue line + an adapter check is tracked as a backlog issue in `agent-skills-workflow` — not built here."
}
```

```json
{
  "team": "c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1",
  "title": "Adapter: surface node `delegates_to` as an on-pickup instruction on the emitted issue",
  "state": "Backlog",
  "labels": [
    "8d85dbb0"
  ],
  "description": "**Repo:** `agent-skills-workflow` (the tracker-binding adapter). Not built in `agent-skills-shaper` — that repo's contract is tool-agnostic by rule and names no tracker.\n\n**Why:** `delivery-shape` emits a delivery plan whose every node carries a `delegates_to` front-matter key (the discipline that owns the node — `planning-and-task-breakdown` for build stories, `product-spike`/`skill-creator`/`design-doc`/etc. otherwise, or \"ops slot / no breakdown step\" for `ktlo`). That key fires at **build time, when the issue is picked up** — not during plan emission. The picking-up agent is told to honour it by the consumer rule (`rules/linear-workflow.md` → *On start*). For that instruction to reach the agent, the adapter that renders the plan into the concrete tracker must **surface each node's `delegates_to` on the emitted issue**.\n\n**Scope:**\n- Read each node's `delegates_to` from the walk-delivery-plan manifest (already carried in both `--text` and `--json` output of `bin/walk-delivery-plan`).\n- Render it into the emitted issue as an `▶ On pickup` instruction naming the delegate skill to invoke before coding.\n- A `ktlo` node renders \"no breakdown step\" (roadmap A5 carve-out — no delegate to fire).\n- Add an adapter check that **fails if any non-ktlo emitted issue lacks the on-pickup line.**\n\n**Done when:** running the adapter over the worked example emits issues each carrying the correct `▶ On pickup` line, and the adapter check fails loudly on a hand-broken case (a non-ktlo issue with the line stripped).\n\n**References:**\n- Producer spec: `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` § *Delegation — timing & surfacing* (the obligation this issue discharges).\n- Manifest source: `bin/walk-delivery-plan` (carries `delegates_to` per issue; `--json` for programmatic consumption).\n- Target output (worked example, hand-produced): the six D2/D3 issues ABA-285…290, each of which already carries the `▶ On pickup` line by hand."
}
```

> AGENT

Plan fully executed. Summary:

**In-repo (commit `8d6ed29`, pushed to main):**
- **`docs/delivery-shape-contract.md`** — marked `delegates_to` required on the schema line; added the **Delegation — timing & surfacing** section (tool-agnostic, names no tracker). This is the canonical home for the deferred-delegation semantics.
- **`bin/walk-delivery-plan`** — `delegates_to` added to `REQUIRED["node"]` (gate now exits 2 if any node omits it), carried into the manifest, and rendered per issue. The existing gate is now the producer-side fitness function.
- **`rules/linear-workflow.md`** (consumer keystone) + **`AGENTS.md`** (mirror) — *On start* now tells every picking-up agent to invoke the named delegate before coding; ktlo issues carry no breakdown step.

**Verification:**
- Walk-script on the worked example → exit 0, `Oracle 3 / 9 / 19 → MATCH`, every issue renders a `delegates_to:` line.
- Gate proof on a scratch copy: stripping a node's `delegates_to` → exit 2, `node missing required front-matter ['delegates_to']`. The fitness function bites.
- Contract section confirmed to name no tracker (Linear/Jira/GitHub/Asana all absent).

**Linear:**
- **ABA-285** — third "Done when" added (delegates_to enforced by walk-script; emitted-issue guidance documents deferred delegation).
- **ABA-287** — pinned comment repointed to cite the contract section as the durable home, listing all three enforced layers + the deferred seam.
- **ABA-291** (new, Backlog, `repo:agent-skills-workflow`) — adapter issue to render `delegates_to` into the concrete `▶ On pickup` line + an adapter check; deferred to the adapter repo.

The rule is now encoded by construction across producer spec, mechanical gate, and always-loaded consumer rule — so every future shaped project inherits it without relying on skill prose to remember.
